# P4.9.10-A1 — debug/admin guard conventions inspection

Date: 2026-05-18
Status: GENERATED

## Purpose

Inspect existing project conventions before hardening debug object-cloud routes.

Routes under review:

- /api/value-objects/debug/cloud-profile
- /value-objects/debug/cloud-profile

## Questions

- Does the project already have admin/dev guard helpers?
- Does the project already use role fields or admin flags?
- Does the project already use NODE_ENV / VERCEL_ENV debug gates?
- Should P4.9.10 add a small local guard or a shared helper?

## Current recommendation before code changes

If no existing admin/role guard is found, the safest P4.9.10 implementation should be minimal:

1. Keep route names under /debug/.
2. Add explicit debug exposure guard to the API endpoint.
3. Allow in local development by default.
4. Require an explicit env flag for production-like environments.
5. Keep UI page hidden from navigation.

Possible env flag name:

- VALUE_OBJECT_CLOUD_DEBUG_ENABLED=true

Possible guard rule:

- allow if NODE_ENV !== production
- allow if VALUE_OBJECT_CLOUD_DEBUG_ENABLED === true
- otherwise return 403
